# Annex A — Foods by BRS and Cross-BRS

Baseline: published `main` at `87b8f11d` (pull request #229). RSP is a reporting calculation. It is not an approved production ranking. A food is listed when an admitted dietary input (`dietary_input: true`) resolves to that food, or when a registered one-hop related-substance route reaches an admitted target and the food has no direct observation of that target. The quantity on a related row is the intermediate substance.

RSP = 100 × food concentration / highest verified point concentration in the same substance, unit, basis and food form. Dry-form records (dried, dehydrated, powder, flour, or mature seeds) are not ranked against foods recorded as prepared or fresh. Measurement spans stay unranked. Presence-only records stay unranked. A blank basis does not receive an RSP. The provisional reporting filter keeps at most five foods with RSP at least 10 and does not fill empty places. A sole source is the only verified point in that group; its RSP of 100 is not a comparison with foods on another basis.
